residue on the copied key useful but insufficient evidence?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. It can suggest a copying method without uniquely identifying the person responsible.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. It identifies the machine that caused every reported illness.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. It shows that the original maintenance log must be false.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. It proves that the elected workers organized the copying.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"35\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"37\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v04c02","title":"The Boat with Two Names","volume":"卷四｜盐与钢的王冠","anchor":"chapter-0038","paragraphs":["The vessel leaving the harbor was too small to carry the cargo described in the invoice. Mara watched it through the scratched telescope. From the station roof, its white cabin looked no larger than a sugar cube. A sailor stood on the stern beside an orange balloon that bounced against a short antenna. There was no crane, no industrial loading gate, and no room for nineteen sealed cells.","\"A diversion,\" Cassian said.","\"Possibly. Or we are looking for the wrong kind of cell.\"","He accepted the correction without argument. That, more than his title, had begun to make him dangerous to her peace of mind.","Ivo arrived carrying a portable tool case, a tape-measure, and the expression of a man who had already decided to object to the weather. He had spent the morning at the hospital arguing with an electrical contractor. Now somebody expected him to board a boat. His opinion of the day was falling fast.","\"I have a day-job,\" he informed them. \"It involves machines that stay where I put them.\"","\"You also have a remarkable talent for identifying a prototype from a photograph,\" Mara said.","\"Flattery is a form of exploitation.\"","\"We have peanuts.\"","He took the bag. \"I am willing to incur some economic risk.\"","Leah's cousin had agreed to lend them a fishing boat. It lay beyond the beach, where thirteen wooden steps descended from a shabby shop. Its price tag was written in chalk: CHARTER, ASK INSIDE. Underneath, someone had added, FOR PEOPLE WHO UNDERSTAND THAT A BOAT IS NOT AN AMBULANCE.","The cousin, a wealthy-looking woman with an unwealthy-looking cigarette, introduced herself as Captain Rook. She had once been a naval electrician. After a wage dispute she had left the service, bought the boat, and discovered that commerce could be as strenuous as war. Her little business now provided work for two local laborers and a retired typist who handled the weekly accounts.","\"Absolute cost, including fuel and my time,\" she said, naming a figure.","Mara looked at Cassian. \"Do we have that?\"","\"We do.\"","\"Do you mean we have it or you have it?\"","He paused. Rook looked delighted.","\"A recoverable investigation expenditure,\" Cassian said.","\"Put that in writing.\"","\"Of course.\"","Rook produced a pen with the speed of a pirate drawing a pistol. \"I like her.\"","They departed before the security team reached the roof. At noon the coast lay behind them in a pale strip. Downtown Asterfall rose above it, fashionable towers sheltering a multitude of homes whose residents could scarcely afford the commute. Rook pointed to a new housing development on the slope. Half its apartments stood empty, while the city was said to be suffering a housing crisis.","\"A curious form of scarcity,\" Mara said.","\"The owners prefer investment returns to tenants,\" Rook replied. \"An economist explained it to me. I understood the explanation. I still disapprove.\"","On the cabin wall hung a faded photograph of Rook as a young woman, standing beside a Japanese steamer. A small rubber goose was fixed to the photograph's corner. It wore a paper diamond crown.","\"My daughter's idea,\" Rook said, catching Mara's glance. \"I was away for her school play. She said a princess required a mother with some dignity.\"","\"And the goose?\"","\"Her editorial judgment.\"","The white vessel had turned southeast. Rook kept her distance, using a fleet of tourist boats to conceal their course. The afternoon current carried them towards a broad tanker anchored beyond the regular shipping channel. Beside its immense shell, the white boat appeared small enough to fit in a lounge.","Ivo bent over the photograph of the hospital unit. He had ignored the outside view for twenty minutes, a feat Mara admired until she noticed his white knuckles.","\"You do not have to look,\" she said quietly.","\"I know. I am proving it.\"","She sat beside him. The companionship seemed to help more than reassurance would have.","\"This housing is industrial,\" Ivo said at last, tapping the image. \"But the mechanism isn't designed for manufacturing. See the ventilation slots? An electronic assembly that gives off that much heat needs a fan. There isn't one.\"","\"A different form of cooling?\"","\"Or the heat specification is false. I cannot conclude which from a photo.\"","Mara found herself smiling. \"Noor would like that answer.\"","\"Noor would demand a questionnaire, a random sample, and my childhood medical records.\"","The joke could not eliminate his fear, but it restored some of his usual forthright manner. He opened the tool case and took out a small detector he had made from a damaged radio. He had refused to call it a finished product. It was a prototype, he insisted, and a prototype came with no warranty against embarrassment.","As they drew near the tanker, the detector began to click.","Rook cut the motor. The silence was broken by a roar of machinery from the larger ship. Men in plain coveralls were lowering an empty carriage onto the white boat. The movement revealed a painted name beneath its stern. Mara compared it with the name on her chart.","\"It's been rewritten.\"","Cassian raised the telescope. \"Two registrations?\"","\"Or a sale that hasn't reached the records. A changed name doesn't establish an illegal transfer.\"","\"But a deliberately concealed one might.\"","An object dropped from the crane. It struck the water without a splash large enough to attract the crew's attention. At first Mara took it for a scrap of textile. Then it moved against the current.","\"There!\"","Rook swung an oar over the side. Cassian caught the floating material with its blade. It was a sponge tied around a sealed plastic case. Within the case lay a folded sheet of paper and a small silver circuit board.","Somebody on the tanker was trying to convey information without being seen.","The paper contained a repeated list of numbers. One column was marked POWER. Another was marked PEOPLE. Between them, in hurried handwriting, someone had written: DO NOT COMBINE THE TESTS.","Mara's excitement faded into something colder. The list might refer to power failures and staffing figures. It might refer to the equipment itself. She could not legitimately choose the most frightening explanation simply because it fit her fears.","Cassian read the page once, then again. \"We need whoever wrote this.\"","From the tanker came a short whistle. A young man at a porthole raised one hand. A second man appeared behind him. The hand disappeared.","Rook restarted the motor.","\"Stay out of the way,\" she told Ivo as she accelerated.","\"That is my predominant nautical skill.\"","Mara reached for the little boat's ladder. Cassian caught her wrist, stopped, and let go before she had to ask.","\"Tell me the plan,\" he said.","It was such a small change that it almost undid her. Once he would have given her an instruction. Now he waited while the boat rocked and a stranger was dragged from a window.","\"We cannot board a tanker with four people and a bag of peanuts. We can make them believe that something more complicated is happening.\"","She took June's press card from the pocket of her coat. June had loaned it to her with an elaborate warning about exploitation, impersonation, and stains. The card would not make her a reporter. It might make the tanker captain reluctant to be caught treating her as an intruder.","\"If they call law enforcement?\" Ivo asked.","\"We will receive company.\"","\"And if they don't?\"","\"We will know something about their priorities.\"","Rook sounded the horn. Mara stood in the bow, held up the card, and announced that the investigation was being recorded for nationwide publication. The announcement was only partly true. Her phone was recording. No newspaper had agreed to publish anything.","The distinction mattered. She would have to explain it later.","On the tanker, a man in a spotless jacket appeared at the rail. He looked neither surprised nor particularly concerned.","\"Miss Vale,\" he called. \"Lady Ardent hoped you would come.\"","Behind Mara, Cassian's hand settled briefly at the small of her back. He did not push her forward or pull her away. He was simply there.","For the first time since the boat left the shore, she was grateful for the weight of the hospital's little silver flower inside her coat. She had brought it because it reminded her of her mother. She had not yet told anyone that, whenever the detector clicked, the flower's metal petals moved."],"questions":[{"type":"细节","stem":"Why does the note thrown from the ship disturb Mara?","options":["Its handwriting proves that Elena is aboard the ship.","It tells her that the ship has lost its entire crew.","It contains a bill that the hospital has already paid.","Its figures may concern people, although the equipment alone cannot establish that interpretation."],"answer":3,"explanation":"便条中的统计也可能涉及参与者；Mara没有把这种可怕解释直接当成已证实的事实。","evidence":[51],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1407,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0038\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">卷四｜盐与钢的王冠</div><h1>The Boat with Two Names</h1><p id=\"chapter-0038-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-9016\" data-words=\"9016\">vessel</a> leaving <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3809\" data-words=\"3809\">harbor</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-792\" data-words=\"792\">was</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8567\" data-words=\"8567\">too</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7661\" data-words=\"7661\">small</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8538\" data-words=\"8538\">to</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1275\" data-words=\"1275\">carry</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1269\" data-words=\"1269\">cargo</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-2268\" data-words=\"2268\">described</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4167\" data-words=\"4167\">in</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> invoice. Its handwriting proves that Elena is aboard the ship.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. It tells her that the ship has lost its entire crew.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. Ivo looked at it, looked at the cabin they had just left, and said a word that Mara suspected was missing from every polite dictionary.","\"You may stay with Rook,\" Cassian told him.","\"The circuit board is going up there.\"","\"It can come back down.\"","Ivo took the case from Mara. \"That is what I am afraid of.\"","He climbed. Mara followed close enough to hear the uneven rhythm of his breathing. At the top, she put a hand on his shoulder. He did not look at her, but the rigid line of his back eased a little. Courage, she thought, was badly represented by the men who spoke most confidently about it. Sometimes it consisted of taking one more step while desperately wishing for a bed on solid ground.","Their host introduced himself as Director Pell. He wore a cheap digital watch and an expensive expression. Behind him, the ship's immense cable reels rose like the foundations of an unfinished town. A generator hummed beneath the deck. To the north, aircraft descended towards the city's airport; farther out, a cruise liner carried its bright, floating marketplace towards the gulf.","\"We operate a business-friendly vessel,\" Pell said. \"I hope your visit will reflect that.\"","\"I shall try to look commercially approachable,\" Mara replied.","He led them past a storage bay. A steel bolt had been welded across one door. Another stood open, revealing crates of electrical hardware, medical sample carriers, and a mechanized trolley. The combination was interesting without being conclusive. A ship could transport legitimate goods for several customers at once.","\"You are looking for the missing hospital equipment,\" Pell said. \"It was delivered. Your hospital sold the lease.\"","\"Our hospital did not own the lease,\" Mara said.","\"Ownership is a complex matter.\"","\"That is why I would like to see the contract.\"","He smiled. \"Lady Ardent anticipated that.\"","The contract lay in a glass-fronted office whose broad windows overlooked the sea. An anchor pendant served as a paperweight. On the wall, a framed photograph showed the launch of a commercial spacecraft. Beneath it hung a much smaller picture of an electrician kneeling beside an old power plant. Pell noticed Mara comparing them.","\"Lady Ardent likes to remind her investors that technology grows from ordinary work.\"","\"Does she pay the ordinary workers?\"","\"At a competitive rate.\"","\"That was not the question.\"","Cassian sat beside her. He placed his notebook on the desk but did not open it. Pell's gaze lingered on the notebook, then moved to the folded paper Mara held.","\"A note thrown from a window,\" he said. \"A dramatic means of communication. One must consider the writer's reliability.\"","\"We are trying to do precisely that. Where is he?\"","\"Resting.\"","\"I would like to hear him say so.\"","For the first time Pell's smile looked effortful. He pressed a button. A screen in the corner lit up, showing Selene Ardent seated in a pale room beside a bowl of northern spring flowers.","\"Mara,\" she said. \"You have made considerable progress.\"","The informality was deliberate. It invited Mara to accept a relationship whose terms she had never agreed to.","\"We have found a man who may need help.\"","\"You have found a man who breached a conditional employment agreement. Those are not necessarily the same thing.\"","\"Then you will have no objection to an independent interview.\"","Selene folded her hands. A diamond on her little finger caught the light. Mara disliked how much she wanted to steal it.","\"I admire your conviction,\" Selene said. \"I question the economy of your methods. You spend days chasing individual invoices when I could give you the means to transform an entire district.\"","On the desk, Pell opened a portfolio. It contained an offer to finance Mercy House, restore the old theater, cancel certain debts, and establish a training program for local workers. The figures were not decorative. With that money, Noor could replace equipment that failed during surgery. The landlord could repair the roof without raising the rent. Ivo could hire apprentices instead of working through the night.","For one dangerous moment, Mara saw the whole bright future at once.","\"In response to your public concerns,\" Selene continued, \"we are willing to fund an independent advisory group. You could be in charge.\"","\"Independent of whom?\"","\"The day-to-day administration.\"","Mara read the conditions. The group could advise but not publish findings without prior approval. Its access to raw records would be restricted by commercial confidentiality. Any disagreement would first go to an arbitrator selected by the financial institution that managed the investment.","\"You have offered me a voice,\" she said. \"And reserved the right to decide whether anybody hears it.\"","\"You prefer no resources at all?\"","That was the trap: a choice presented as exhaustive when it was merely convenient to the person presenting it. Mara could reject these conditions without rejecting every form of investment. Yet the distinction did not make the hospital's needs disappear.","\"I prefer an offer whose generosity does not depend on our silence.\"","\"You misread me.\"","\"Then correct the contract.\"","Beside her, Cassian's hand shifted on the desk. He had not spoken. She knew what the figures meant to him too. He had seen Noor counting clean sheets and Ivo deciding which machine could wait. His refusal to make the choice for her felt less like protection than trust, and required more from her.","Selene looked towards someone beyond the camera. \"The meeting seems unlikely to proceed usefully. Director Pell will show you the equipment. Then perhaps we can begin again.\"","The screen went dark.","Pell led them below. The young man from the porthole sat in a narrow room with a cup of untouched tea. His name was Ewan. He had signed a consent form permitting tests of a new workplace fatigue monitor. According to Pell, his participation was voluntary and paid.","\"Can you leave?\" Mara asked.","Ewan glanced at the doorway. \"At the end of my shift.\"","\"When is that?\"","\"They changed it.\"","Pell called the change an administrative delay. Ewan called it a week. The words referred to the same period and conveyed very different judgments about it.","Ivo requested the technical manual. Pell handed over a tablet. The manual described the assembly as economical, reproducible, and capable of reducing wasteful downtime. It did not explain why the medical sample carriers stood beside it, or why the participant information sheet referred to a different version of the apparatus.","\"The two documents may have been issued at different stages,\" Ivo said. \"I need the revision history.\"","\"You are not qualified to perform a clinical review.\"","\"No. I am qualified to notice that these two circuit diagrams do not describe the same machine.\"","Pell reached for the tablet. Cassian moved between them. He did not touch Pell. Somehow that made the movement more final.","\"We will preserve both versions,\" he said.","Mara felt the silver flower stir inside her coat. She took it out. One petal had turned clockwise, aligning itself with a cable beneath Ewan's chair. The flower did not respond to the man. When she moved it towards the wall, it followed the cable's course instead.","An instrument, she thought. Her mother had left her an instrument disguised as an ornament.","\"Where does that cable go?\" she asked.","Pell did not answer.","The detector in Ivo's hand gave a sudden, piercing cry. Ewan covered his ears. Cassian caught the edge of the table, his face drained of color.","Mara saw him reach for a memory that was not there.","\"Cassian?\"","He looked at her without recognition for less than a second. It was enough.","Then he said her name. The effort in the single word frightened her more than an admission of pain would have.","Pell stepped towards the control panel.","Mara took the tablet from his hand and let it fall. Glass shattered across the deck. Everyone looked down except Ivo, who was watching the cable.","\"The red switch,\" she said.","He pulled it.","The machinery stopped. In the silence that followed, Ewan began to cry.","Above them, an alarm rang. A voice announced that the ship was leaving its anchorage.","Director Pell looked at the broken tablet, then at Mara. His expression had lost every trace of hospitality.","\"You owe us a considerable sum,\" he said.","Mara stepped in front of Cassian.","\"Put it on my account.\""],"questions":[{"type":"论证结构","stem":"The offer to finance Mercy House is presented as a trap chiefly because it","options":["contains no money that could be useful to the hospital.","depends on Mara being unable to read the contract.","requires the hospital to close before receiving any payment.","makes acceptance and total abandonment appear to be the only possible choices."],"answer":3,"explanation":"Pell把两种选项包装成全部可能，压缩Mara考虑其他途径的空间。这是文中明确指出的假两难。","evidence":[42],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1409,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0039\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">卷四｜盐与钢的王冠</div><h1>A Very Reasonable Offer</h1><p id=\"chapter-0039-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> tanker&#x27;s <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3999\" data-words=\"3999\">hospitality</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-896\" data-words=\"896\">began</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-9260\" data-words=\"9260\">with</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1\" data-words=\"1\">a</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5191\" data-words=\"5191\">metal</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4700\" data-words=\"4700\">ladder</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8383\" data-words=\"8383\">that</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8206\" data-words=\"8206\">swayed</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-47\" data-words=\"47\">above</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-1\" data-words=\"1\">a</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1014\" data-words=\"1014\">blue</a>, <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4286\" data-words=\"4286\">indifferent</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5661\" data-words=\"5661\">ocean</a>. Ivo placed a heavy wrench across the open casing. It was a straightforward obstacle. Whatever sophisticated technology the assembly contained, its cover could no longer close.","\"And we are not turning that on again,\" Ivo added.","Beyond the little room, feet hurried across the deck. The crew had heard the alarm. Some were running towards the engine compartment; others were waiting for instructions from a man whose only instruction was to keep everyone where they were. An emergency could reveal the difference between authority and competence with regrettable speed.","Mara held out her hand to Ewan. He stared at it as if she had offered something he could not afford.","\"I will have to pay for the test,\" he said.","\"We can discuss the bill on land.\"","\"They said my allowance—\"","\"Your allowance does not buy your right to leave.\"","Pell called that a legal interpretation. Cassian looked up. His face was grey, but his voice had recovered its accustomed precision.","\"Then you can contest it before a court. Until then, the participant has withdrawn consent.\"","For once Pell had no immediate comment. Mara did not mistake his silence for agreement. He was choosing which part of the problem to address first.","The ship lurched. Somewhere above them, a siren began to sound over the original alarm. The voices on the public-address system overlapped. An order to remain calm was followed by an order to clear the lower passage. Mara could smell hot insulation.","\"That isn't our fault,\" Ivo said, listening.","\"Can you be certain?\"","\"No. But cutting this circuit should reduce the load, not increase it. I need to see the main distribution board.\"","Pell moved to block the doorway. \"You are not authorized—\"","\"You can authorize a fireman instead, if you have one available.\"","A woman in a navy uniform appeared behind him. Her badge said CHIEF ENGINEER. She looked from the disabled machine to Ewan's pale face, then to the smoke beginning to collect near the ceiling.","\"Move,\" she told Pell.","It was not a request.","Mara helped Ewan into the passage. Cassian followed, one hand on the wall. She wanted to put her arm around him and hated that the movement might make him feel watched. He solved the problem by taking her elbow.","\"Steady me,\" he said.","The words were quiet. She thought he knew what they cost her to hear, and what they gave her in return.","The chief engineer's name was Anika. She had been told that the medical unit drew less electricity than an ordinary kitchen appliance. The actual voltage readings were now fluctuating across two displays. She pointed to a freshly installed extension cable.","\"That is not in the approved programme.\"","\"It isn't in either version of the manual,\" Ivo said. \"The hardware has been changed after inspection.\"","They could infer a discrepancy. They could not yet infer who had ordered it, whether it explained Ewan's condition, or why the ship had begun to move. Mara kept those questions apart. It was tempting, when the truth seemed within reach, to let curiosity turn every separate fact into one compelling revelation.","Anika ordered the generator isolated. Pell protested that the shutdown would interrupt a costly trial. She asked whether the trial was worth the ship. He demanded to speak privately. She refused.","\"I have seventy people aboard,\" she said. \"Your confidential equipment can wait.\"","Above them the motor changed pitch. For a moment the floor seemed to lift. Then the tanker settled into a slower, uneven movement. The alarm ceased, leaving the clatter of unsecured plates in the galley and the distant chatter of a radio.","The engineer had prevented an immediate failure. That did not mean the vessel was safe. A preceding fault had already damaged a steering component, and the crew could not tell whether the backup system would hold. Anika called for a tug. The harbor replied that the first available vessel was forty minutes away.","\"Then we have forty minutes to improve our position,\" she said.","Mara liked her immediately.","She settled Ewan on a bench beneath a skylight. A steward brought water and a thick blanket. Nobody asked him to make a formal statement. Noor, reached by telephone, asked whether he could remain awake and answer simple questions. She did not offer a diagnosis over a noisy connection. She requested an ambulance at the harbor and told Mara to keep the devices away from him until they could be independently assessed.","\"And Cassian?\" she asked.","\"He heard the alarm and became disoriented.\"","Cassian reached for the telephone. \"I am present.\"","\"Your presence is encouraging. Your previous habit of treating a symptom as a nuisance is not. Sit down.\"","Mara smiled in spite of herself. He obeyed with a dignity that made the obedience unexpectedly endearing.","She sat beside him. In front of them, the broken tablet lay inside a clear evidence bag. She could see her face reflected in one surviving corner. A thief had spent years learning how to take things without leaving a mark. Now every mark mattered.","\"The sound,\" Cassian said. \"I heard it at the old accident site.\"","She waited.","\"I did not forget you. For a moment, I remembered being somewhere else.\"","The distinction loosened something painful inside her. \"We do not have to decide what it signifies now.\"","\"I would like you to know it.\"","\"I do.\"","He looked at the silver flower in her hand. \"And that?\"","\"A detector, I think. A very limited one. It follows a current in a nearby cable. I thought the movement was coincidence before.\"","\"Your mother made it?\"","\"She restored it. The mechanism is older. Her notebook refers to a model from the late 1970s. I assumed the drawings were an artistic preoccupation.\"","\"They may still be partly that.\"","She turned the flower clockwise. \"Yes. I keep forgetting that a thing can have more than one purpose.\"","Outside the porthole, the sea was changing color. Clouds had compressed into a low grey shelf above the western horizon. Rook's boat rose and fell beside the tanker, its little radio antenna drawing an unsteady line against the sky.","Mara went to the rail and waved. Rook held up a hand. Five fingers, then four. A countdown to something the wind prevented her from hearing.","A crack sounded below. The loading door, left partly open, swung against its restraint. Water rushed across the lower deck. Ewan's blanket slipped from the bench.","Ivo seized the door's manual wheel. Anika caught the other side. The wheel moved a quarter turn and jammed.","\"A polymer seal has folded into the groove,\" Ivo said. \"We need a thin plate.\"","Pell had vanished. The steward ran for the galley. Mara took the metal cover from an emergency instruction board and slid it beneath the wheel. It was too thick. She tried again, changing the angle.","\"Stop,\" Cassian said.","For one sharp second she was indignant. Then she saw what he had seen: a cable drawn taut across the floor, directly behind her heel.","He did not tell her to leave. He held the cable clear while she knelt.","The metal slipped into the groove. Ivo turned the wheel. The seal moved, caught, and finally gave way. Water heaved against the door once more, but this time the door held.","Mara stayed on her knees, laughing in a small, breathless way that had nothing to do with amusement.","Cassian crouched beside her. \"Are you hurt?\"","\"Only my pride. I expected to look more elegant.\"","\"I shall amend the official record.\"","She caught his sleeve before he could stand. For a moment neither moved. Around them the crew worked, the radio hissed, and the ship continued its slow, uncertain passage towards the coast. The world had not made room for a declaration. It had given them a narrow place in which to keep each other alive.","Anika returned with the maintenance handbook. One page had been removed. In its place somebody had inserted a freight manifest.
